Sem conector, trabalha com o anúncio colado; com o navegador ligado e a linha `linkedin (logado):` no perfil, busca primeiro dentro da conta dele — e sem… ([kapstanhq/oficina](https://github.com/kapstanhq/oficina/tree/HEAD/vagas/skills/buscar-vagas) · ★1)

- **busco**: Run BUSCO completeness assessment on genomes, transcriptomes, or predicted proteomes. Generates conda env setup, shared-lineage download, SLURM array scripts with sample-sheet-driven (input × lineage) fan-out, a local single-input fallback, and an aggregator. Use when running BUSCO, assessing genome/transcriptome/proteome completeness, scoring against an odb10 lineage, or when the user mentions "BUSCO", "BUSCO score", "completeness", "C/S/D/F/M", "ortholog completeness", "genome/transcriptome/assembly QC", or asks whether an assembly is complete. Sanitizes FASTA headers automatically — BUSCO… ([MusserLab/lab-claude-skills](https://github.com/MusserLab/lab-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/genome-annotation/skills/busco) · ★6)
- **busco-assessment**: Use when running BUSCO completeness assessment for genome, transcriptome, or protein FASTA files, including offline lineage selection from public_data/BUSCO_DB and Slurm-backed execution. Do not use this skill for general genome statistics such as N50 or BUSCO-unrelated annotation checks. ([biojiayuxin/potato-agent](https://github.com/biojiayuxin/potato-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/potato-knowledge-bioinformatics/busco-assessment) · ★1)
- **busco-assessor**: Genome, transcriptome, and protein completeness assessment via BUSCO v6. Agentic lineage routing from organism description, all three BUSCO modes, auto-lineage support, and full demo mode without the BUSCO binary. ([ClawBio/ClawBio](https://github.com/ClawBio/ClawBio/tree/HEAD/skills/busco-assessor) · ★1.2k · 4 copies)
- **busco-automated-lineage-selection**: Use when binned contig FASTA files (MAGs) from metagenome binning need per-bin completeness and contamination estimates and assembly QC metrics (contig count, total length, N50). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/busco-automated-lineage-selection))
- **busco-completeness-assessment**: Use when scoring de novo diploid assemblies built from PacBio CLR, Nanopore R9 or Nanopore R10 reads, where completeness (BUSCO) must be reported next to NG50, phase block NG50 and Hamming error to compare pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/busco-completeness-assessment--c23c0cabfbdd2bf8))
- **busco-completeness-assessment**: Use when a newly assembled genome (e.g. from an overlap-layout-consensus long-read assembler) needs a quantitative completeness/quality score comparable across assemblers, using a curated ortholog lineage database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/busco-completeness-assessment--d5145f955f8f4594))
- **busco-completeness-assessment**: Use when benchmarking a de novo transcriptome assembly (e.g. Trinity-assembled H. subflexa pheromone gland) with BUSCO ortholog counts, especially to verify that chimera/redundancy filtering reduces duplicated BUSCO groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/busco-completeness-assessment))
- **busco-completeness-interpretation**: 当需要解读 BUSCO 完整度结果（C/S/D/F/M/n）、跨基因组/转录组/蛋白组比较完整度、或撰写可发表的质控报告时使用；做谱系数据集选型、正确按 S+D 计完整度、解析 short_summary 与 full_table.tsv，产出可比的完整度统计与解读结论；不适用于跑变异检测（用 gatk-variant-calling）、读写 BAM/VCF（用 genomic-file-toolkit）、reads 级质控（用 fastp-fastq-preprocessing）。触发词：BUSCO、完整度、completeness、Duplicated、单拷贝、Fragmented、Missing、odb10、谱系数据集、组装质量、proteome ([findscripter/everything-skills](https://github.com/findscripter/everything-skills/tree/HEAD/09-verticals/busco-completeness-interpretation) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **busco-gene-completeness-assessment**: Use when comparing assemblies of downsampled (~5x) HiFi human datasets — e.g. HiFiCCL-Hifiasm vs hifiasm --primary — to verify that reduced misassembled contig length does not cost single-copy ortholog completeness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/busco-gene-completeness-assessment))
- **busco-gene-completeness-evaluation**: Use when comparing multiple genome-guided transcriptome assembly strategies and you need to decide which is most complete: e.g., choosing between long-read-only (IsoSeq PacBio), hybrid merged BAM, and short-read-only assemblies using BUSCO lineage scores plus Ex90N50. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/busco-gene-completeness-evaluation))
- **busco-phylogeny**: Generate phylogenies from genome assemblies using BUSCO/compleasm-based single-copy orthologs with scheduler-aware workflow generation ([aiskillstore/marketplace](https://github.com/aiskillstore/marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/brunoasm/busco-phylogeny) · ★487 · 8 copies)
- **busco-quality-threshold-filtering**: Decision support for routing metagenome-assembled genome (MAG) FASTA bins between GTDB-Tk and CAT/BAT during the taxonomic annotation stage of nf-core/mag, using BUSCO quality summaries as the gate. - **busco-single-copy-ortholog-profiling**: Use when you need to profile completeness of MAGs via universal single-copy orthologs, or detect systematic absence of USCO genes (e.g. purine biosynthesis) across a clade of genomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/busco-single-copy-ortholog-profiling))
- **busco-status-interpretation**: Guide to interpreting BUSCO completeness statuses: why Duplicated BUSCOs count as complete, parsing output files, computing/comparing completeness across proteomes/genomes, common counting mistakes. Use when running BUSCO QC, comparing assemblies, or reporting completeness. See also: prokka-genome-annotation for annotation workflows feeding BUSCO. ([jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills](https://github.com/jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genomics-bioinformatics/qc/busco-status-interpretation) · ★394 · 5 copies)
- **busco-status-interpretation**: Guide to correctly interpreting BUSCO (Benchmarking Universal Single-Copy Orthologs) completeness statuses. Covers why Duplicated BUSCOs count as complete, how to parse BUSCO output files, how to compute and compare completeness across proteomes and genomes, and common counting mistakes. Relevant when running genome or proteome quality assessments with BUSCO, comparing assemblies, or reporting completeness statistics. See also: prokka-genome-annotation for annotation workflows that feed into BUSCO assessment. ([sulfierry/mcp](https://github.com/sulfierry/mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/busco-status-interpretation) · ★1)
- **bushiershi**: 仅在用户明确调用 `$bushiershi` 时使用。对抗模型在中文成稿中反复出现的不良表达习惯；生成或改写时识别并清理虚设反面、价值报幕、洞察路标、过程旁白、抽象动作、同义重复、强行升华和磨平原话，让成稿直接、具体、有依据、有推进，同时保留事实、立场、程度、归因和真实不确定性。 ([jinhanbuilds/bushiershi](https://github.com/jinhanbuilds/bushiershi/tree/HEAD/skills/bushiershi) · ★63)
- **business**: Browse, search, and explore your organization's business context system — glossary terms, product catalog, metric definitions, OKRs/objectives, and team structure.
